## Windows native components

Windows builds additionally include the .NET runtime and System.Speech (dotnet/runtime, MIT), the C#/WinRT runtime (microsoft/CsWinRT, MIT), and Windows SDK .NET projections/reference components under Microsoft Windows SDK terms. Native dependency versions are recorded in resources/native-licenses/DEPENDENCIES.json in the installed app.
